	function OnorcStateWisePage(month) {
		/* alert("month="+month);  */
		var year = $("#selectedyear").val();
		if (year == -1) {
			alert("Please Select Year...!");
			return false;
			/* alert("year="+year);  */
		}
		window.location = "stateUnautmated?month=" + month + "&year=" + year;
		return false;
	}
	

</script>

<script>
	$(document).ready(function() {
		
		var path = window.location.pathname

		 if (path.includes("sale/rationCardReport")) {

		        $("#calModal").closest("li").css("display", "none");
		    }
		$('#uname').val('');
		$('#pass').val('');
		$('#ch').val('');
	
		
		// Theme Script
		$("body").on("change",".changeTheme", function(e){
			e.preventDefault();
			//$(".customLogo").toggleClass("active");
			if($(this).val() == "Light"){
				$(".darkLogo").addClass("active");
				$(".lightLogo").removeClass("active");
				$(".itemTitle").removeClass("darkColor");
				$(".state_count_titl h3").removeClass("darkColor");
				$("body").removeClass("dark").addClass("light");
				$("tspan").parent().parent().parent().css("fill","rgb(0,0,0)");
			}else{
				$(".darkLogo").removeClass("active");
				$(".lightLogo").addClass("active");
				$(".itemTitle").addClass("darkColor");
				$(".state_count_titl h3").addClass("darkColor");
				$("body").removeClass("light").addClass("dark");
				$("tspan").parent().parent().parent().css("fill","rgb(255,255,255)");
			}
		});
		// Theme Script
		// Custom Dropdown
		var $myElement = $('.dropdown-toggle');

		  // Click handler for the target element
		  $myElement.on('click', function(event) {
		    event.stopPropagation(); // Prevent click from bubbling up to document
		    $(this).next().toggleClass('show'); // Toggle the 'active' class
		  });
		  $(document).on('click', function() {
		    if ($myElement.next().hasClass('show')) {
		      $myElement.next().removeClass('show'); // Remove 'active' class if it exists
		    }
		  });
		// Custom Dropdown
	});
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  const themeSelector = document.getElementById("themeSelector");

  themeSelector.addEventListener("change", function() {
    const theme = this.value;
    if (theme === "Dark") {
      document.body.style.backgroundColor = "#222";
      document.body.style.color = "#fff";
    } else if (theme === "Light") {
      document.body.style.backgroundColor = "#fff";
      document.body.style.color = "#000";
    }
  });
});

$(document).ready(function() {

  $("#screenreader").click(function() {
    const modal = $(".myModal20");
    modal.show().addClass("show");

    // Focus trap inside modal
    const focusableElements = modal.find('a, button, input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])');
    let firstElem = focusableElements[0];
    let lastElem = focusableElements[focusableElements.length - 1];

    if(firstElem) firstElem.focus();

    // Keydown handler for focus trap & ESC
    modal.on("keydown.focusTrap", function(e) {
      if(e.key === "Tab") {
        if(e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === firstElem) {
          e.preventDefault();
          lastElem.focus();
        } else if(!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === lastElem) {
          e.preventDefault();
          firstElem.focus();
        }
      } else if(e.key === "Escape") {
        closeModal(modal);
      }
    });
  });

  // Close Modal
  $(".close").click(function() {
    const modal = $(this).closest(".myModal20");
    closeModal(modal);
  });

  function closeModal(modal) {
    modal.hide().removeClass("show");
    modal.off("keydown.focusTrap");
    $("#screenreader").focus(); // Return focus to trigger link
  }

  // Enter/Space triggers buttons and role="button"
  $(document).on("keydown", function(e) {
    if((e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") && $(e.target).attr("role") === "button") {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.target.click();
    }
  });

  // Optional: make dropdown toggles respond to Enter/Space
  $(".dropdown-toggle").on("keydown", function(e) {
    if(e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
      e.preventDefault();
      $(this).click();
    }
  });

});
</script>

<script>

  function liveStatesdata() {
		 var url = "liveStatesAjax";
		 $(".loader").show();
		jQuery.ajax({
			type : "GET",
			url : url,
			data : null,
			beforeSend : function() {

			},
			success : function(result) {
				$(".loader").hide();
				$("#liveDivId").html(result);
				//$("#liveDivId").show();
				//$("#myModal11").addClass("show");

			},
			complete : function() {
				$("#liveDivId").show();
				$("#myModal11").addClass("show");
				}
		});

		return false;
	}

  function stateData(stateId) {
	 var url = "stateByCountryAjax";
	 $(".loader").show();
		jQuery.ajax({
			type : "GET",
			url : url,
			data : { "stateCode" :stateId},
			beforeSend : function() {

			},
			success : function(result) {

				$(".loader").hide();
				$("#myModal11").modal("hide");
				$("#stateDivId").html(result);

			},
			complete : function() {

				$("#stateDivId").show();
				$("#stateDefaultDivId").hide();


			}
		});
}

</script>
<!-- currency converter -->
<script>
			$(function() {
				var convertText = $(".convert");
				for(var i=0; i<convertText.length; i++){
					$(".convert").eq(i).text(convert($(".convert").eq(i).text()));
				}
				function convert(text){
					var x = text;
					//var x = $('#result1').text();
					x=x.toString();
					x = x.replace(/\,/g, '');
					var afterPoint = '';
					if(x.indexOf('.') > 0)
					afterPoint = x.substring(x.indexOf('.'),x.length);
					x = Math.floor(x);
					x=x.toString();
					var lastThree = x.substring(x.length-3);
					var otherNumbers = x.substring(0,x.length-3);
					if(otherNumbers != '')
						lastThree = ',' + lastThree;
					var res = otherNumbers.replace(/\B(?=(\d{2})+(?!\d))/g, ",") + lastThree + afterPoint;
					return res;
					//document.getElementById('result1').innerHTML = res;
				}
			});
</script>
<script>
$(function () {
	  var $table = $("table");

	    //$(this).toggleClass("open");
	    $table.find(".customRow").toggleClass("d-none");
	  });
	});
</script>

<script>
$(document).ready(function (e) {
    $('#statecode').on('change', function () {
        ajaxCallDistrictChange();
    });

    $(".menu-toggle").click(function () {
        const $btn = $(this);
        const panelId = $btn.attr("aria-controls");
        const $panel = $("#" + panelId);

        // Toggle panel visibility
        $panel.toggle();

        // Toggle aria-expanded
        const expanded = $btn.attr("aria-expanded") === "true";
        $btn.attr("aria-expanded", !expanded);

        // Toggle icons
        $btn.find("i").toggleClass("d-none");
    });
});

function ajaxCallDistrictChange() {
		var statecode = $("#statecode").val();
		var result;
		$(".loader").show();
		jQuery
				.ajax({
					type : "GET",
					url : "distDetails",
					data : {
						"statecode" : statecode
					},
					beforeSend : function() {
					},
					success : function(result) {
						$(".loader").hide();
						var district = '';
						var district = '<option value=" ">' + 'Select'
								+ '</option>';
						for (var i = 0; i < result.length; i++) {
							district += '<option value="' + result[i].districtcode + '">'
									+ result[i].districtname + '</option>';
						}
						$('#districtcode').empty();
						$('#districtcode').append(district);
						$('#tehsilcode').empty();
					}
				});
	}
$(document).ready(function (e) {
    $('#districtcode').on('change', function () {
        ajaxCallTehsilChange();

    });
});
function ajaxCallTehsilChange() {
	 var statecode = $("#statecode").val();
	 var distcode = $("#districtcode").val();
var result;
$(".loader").show();
jQuery.ajax({
    type : "GET",
    url : "fairPriceShopDetails",
		 data :{
    "statecode" : statecode
       } ,
       beforeSend : function() {
       },
       success : function(result) {
    	   $(".loader").hide();
    	   var tehsil = '<option value=" ">' + 'Select'
								+ '</option>';
						for (var i = 0; i < result.length; i++) {
							if (distcode == result[i].districtCode) {
								tehsil += '<option value="' + result[i].tehsilcode + '">'
										+ result[i].tehsilname + '</option>';
							}
						}
    	   $('#tehsilcode').empty();
    	   $('#tehsilcode').append(tehsil);
    }
});
}

	pop.focus();

	};

async function encrypt(plaintext, key) {
    const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const encodedKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
        'raw',
        encoder.encode(key),
        { name: 'AES-GCM' },
        false,
        ['encrypt']
    );

    const encryptedData = await crypto.subtle.encrypt(
        {
            name: 'AES-GCM',
            iv: iv
        },
        encodedKey,
        encoder.encode(plaintext)
    );

    const combined = new Uint8Array(iv.length + encryptedData.byteLength);
    combined.set(iv);
    combined.set(new Uint8Array(encryptedData), iv.length);

    return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...combined));
}



async function reloadCaptcha(captchaImgId) {
    $.ajax({
        url: 'ReloadCaptcha',
        type: 'POST',
        success: function(response) {
            var captchaImgElement = document.getElementById(captchaImgId);
            if (captchaImgElement && response.captchaBase64) {
                captchaImgElement.src = "data:image/jpg;base64," + response.captchaBase64;
                USER_SALT = response.userSalt;
            } else {
                alert("CAPTCHA reload failed, please try again.");
            }
        },
        error: function() {
            alert("Error occurred while reloading CAPTCHA.");
        }
    });
}

async function validateCaptcha(captcha) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        $.ajax({
            url: 'ValidateCaptcha',
            type: 'POST',
            data: { captcha: captcha },
            success: function(response) {
                if (response.errors && response.errors.invalidCaptchaError) {
                    resolve(false);
                } else {
                    resolve(response.isValid);
                }
            },
            error: function(xhr, status, error) {
                console.error('Failed to validate CAPTCHA:', status, error);
                resolve(false);
            }
        });
    });
}

async function SearchRationCardDetails() {
	var captchaInput = $("#captchaTxt1").val();
	//console.log("captchaInput: " + captchaInput);

	var isCaptchaValid = await validateCaptcha(captchaInput);


	 if (!isCaptchaValid) {
		    // Show SweetAlert error popup with accessible structure
		    swal({
		      content: {
		        element: "div",
		        attributes: {
		          innerHTML: `
		            <div role="alertdialog" aria-labelledby="errorTitle" aria-describedby="errorDesc">
		              <h2 id="errorTitle">Error</h2>
		              <p id="errorDesc">Invalid Captcha. Please try again.</p>
		            </div>`
		        }
		      },
		      icon: "error",
		      buttons: {
		        confirm: { text: "OK", closeModal: true }
		      }
		    });

		    // Wait for modal to render before handling focus
		    setTimeout(() => {
		      const okBtn = document.querySelector(".swal-button");
		      const popup = document.querySelector(".swal-modal");

		      if (!okBtn || !popup) return;
		      okBtn.focus();

		      // Trap focus inside popup
		      const keyHandler = (e) => {
		        if (e.key === "Tab") {
		          e.preventDefault();
		          okBtn.focus();
		        } else if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
		          e.preventDefault();
		          okBtn.click();
		        } else if (e.key === "Escape") {
		          e.preventDefault();
		          okBtn.click();
		        }
		      };

		      const focusInHandler = (e) => {
		        if (!popup.contains(e.target)) {
		          e.preventDefault();
		          okBtn.focus();
		        }
		      };

		      document.addEventListener("keydown", keyHandler, true);
		      document.addEventListener("focusin", focusInHandler, true);

		      // Cleanup after OK click and refocus back to trigger button
		      okBtn.addEventListener(
		        "click",
		        () => {
		          setTimeout(() => {
		            document.removeEventListener("keydown", keyHandler, true);
		            document.removeEventListener("focusin", focusInHandler, true);
		            // Refocus to "Ration Card Transactions" button
		            document.querySelector('[data-bs-target="#rcsearchmyModal"]')?.focus();
		          }, 100);
		        },
		        { once: true }
		      );
		    }, 150);

		    // Clear invalid input
		    $("#captchaTxt1").val("");
		    $("#salercardno").val("");
		    return;
		  }
	var fpsrcardno = $("#fpsrcardno").val();
	var formData = new FormData();
	formData.append("fpsrcardno", fpsrcardno);
	var rctbl;
//var encypted = await encrypt(fpsrcardno,'8qd&Ao5pqC(zGv4P')
$(".loader").show();
jQuery.ajax({
	type : "POST",
	url : "RationCardDetailsfetch",
	data: formData,
	processData: false,
	contentType: false,
   beforeSend : function() {
   },
   success : function(rctbl) {
  	 $(".loader").hide();
  	$('#rationcardTblAAA').empty();
  	$("#RcardId").empty();
  	$("#schemaName").empty();
  	$("#Address").empty();
  	$("#homeStateName").empty();
	$("#respMessage").empty();
  	
  	if(""== rctbl.responsemsg )
   {
  		//alert('Please Wait Your Data is Loaded....'+rctbl.responsemsg)

  		var strTable = "";

   }

			  $("#rcdetailsmyModal")?.modal('show');
			  $('#rationCardFormId')[0]?.reset();
			 // window.location.reload();

},
error: function(xhr, status, error) {
    $(".loader").hide();

    // Optional: inspect response
    console.log("Error:", xhr.responseText);

    // Show meaningful error to user
    swal({
        title: "Invalid Input",
        text: xhr.responseText || "Something went wrong.",
        type: "error",
    });

    // Reset form fields if needed
    $("#captchaTxt1").val("");
    $("#salercardno").val("");
}



});

async function SaleTransactionDetailsPage() {
    var captchaInput = $("#captchaTxt2").val();
    console.log("captchaInput: " + captchaInput);

    var isCaptchaValid = await validateCaptcha(captchaInput);

    if (!isCaptchaValid) {
        // Show SweetAlert error popup with accessible structure
        swal({
          content: {
            element: "div",
            attributes: {
              innerHTML: `
                <div role="alertdialog" aria-labelledby="errorTitle" aria-describedby="errorDesc">
                  <h2 id="errorTitle">Error</h2>
                  <p id="errorDesc">Invalid Captcha. Please try again.</p>
                </div>`
            }
          },
          icon: "error",
          buttons: {
            confirm: { text: "OK", closeModal: true }
          }
        });

        // Wait for modal to render before handling focus
        setTimeout(() => {
          const okBtn = document.querySelector(".swal-button");
          const popup = document.querySelector(".swal-modal");

          if (!okBtn || !popup) return;
          okBtn.focus();

          // Trap focus inside popup
          const keyHandler = (e) => {
            if (e.key === "Tab") {
              e.preventDefault();
              okBtn.focus();
            } else if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
              e.preventDefault();
              okBtn.click();
            } else if (e.key === "Escape") {
              e.preventDefault();
              okBtn.click();
            }
          };

          const focusInHandler = (e) => {
            if (!popup.contains(e.target)) {
              e.preventDefault();
              okBtn.focus();
            }
          };

          document.addEventListener("keydown", keyHandler, true);
          document.addEventListener("focusin", focusInHandler, true);

          // Cleanup after OK click and refocus back to trigger button
          okBtn.addEventListener(
            "click",
            () => {
              setTimeout(() => {
                document.removeEventListener("keydown", keyHandler, true);
                document.removeEventListener("focusin", focusInHandler, true);
                // Refocus to "Ration Card Transactions" button
                document.querySelector('[data-bs-target="#saletransactionmyModal"]')?.focus();
              }, 100);
            },
            { once: true }
          );
        }, 150);

        // Clear invalid input
        $("#captchaTxt2").val("");
        $("#salercardno").val("");
        return;
      }

    var salercardno = $("#salercardno").val();
    var formData = new FormData();
    formData.append("salercardno", salercardno);
    
    $(".loader").show();
    jQuery.ajax({
    	type: "POST",
        url: "saleRationCardDetails",
        data: formData,
        processData: false,
        contentType: false,
        beforeSend: function() {},
        success: function(sltbl) {
            $(".loader").hide();
            $('#saletable').empty();
			
            $("#rationCardNotFound").hide();

            // Check if sltbl is an array and has at least one object
            if (!Array.isArray(sltbl) || sltbl.length === 0) {
                // Show message if no data is found
                $("#rationCardNotFound").text("Ration Card Number is not valid.").show();
                clearFields();
                return;
            }

            if (sltbl[0].ration_card_no === null) {
                // Show message for null ration card number
                $("#rationCardNotFound").text("Ration Card Number is not valid.").show();
                clearFields();
                return;
            }

            // Populate the modal with data
            $("#ration_card_id").text(sltbl[0].ration_card_no);
            $("#Home_stateId").text(sltbl[0].home_state_name);
            $("#sale_state_name_id").text(sltbl[0].sale_state_name);
            $("#schema_Name").text(sltbl[0].scheme_name);

            var strTable = "<tr><th>Member Name</th><th>Sale State</th><th>Rice</th><th>Wheat</th><th>Coarse Grains</th><th>Transaction Date</th></tr>";
            for (var i = 0; i < sltbl.length; i++) {
                strTable += "<tr>";
                strTable += "<td>" + sltbl[i].member_name + "</td>";
                strTable += "<td>" + sltbl[i].sale_state_name + "</td>";
                strTable += "<td>" + sltbl[i].rice + "</td>";
                strTable += "<td>" + sltbl[i].wheat + "</td>";
                strTable += "<td>" + sltbl[i].cg + "</td>";
                strTable += "<td>" + sltbl[i].transaction_date + "</td>";
                strTable += "</tr>";
            }

            $('#saletable').append(strTable);
            $("#saletransactionmyModal").modal('hide');
            $("#saletransactiondetailsmyModal").modal('show');
            $('#saletransactionmodelId')[0].reset();
        },
        error: function(xhr, status, error) {
            $(".loader").hide();

            // Optional: inspect response
            console.log("Error:", xhr.responseText);

            // Show meaningful error to user
            swal({
                title: "Invalid Input",
                text: xhr.responseText || "Something went wrong.",
                type: "error",
            });

            // Reset form fields if needed
            $("#captchaTxt2").val("");
            $("#salercardno").val("");
        }
    });
}

function clearFields() {
    // Clear all data fields
    $("#ration_card_id").text('');
    $("#Home_stateId").text('');
    $("#sale_state_name_id").text('');
    $("#schema_Name").text('');
    $('#saletable').empty();
    $("#saletransactionmyModal").modal('hide');
    $("#saletransactiondetailsmyModal").modal('show');
}

</script>
<!--fix modal accessibility with JavaScript  -->
<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
  const modal = document.getElementById("myModal11"); 
  const modalContent = modal.querySelector(".modal-content");
  let lastFocusedElement;
  modal.addEventListener("shown.bs.modal", function () {
    lastFocusedElement = document.activeElement; 
    const focusableElements = modalContent.querySelectorAll(
      'a[href], area[href], input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), ' +
      'textarea:not([disabled]), button:not([disabled]), iframe, object, embed, ' +
      '[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"]), [contenteditable="true"]'
    );
    const firstFocusable = focusableElements[0];
    const lastFocusable = focusableElements[focusableElements.length - 1];
    if (firstFocusable) firstFocusable.focus();
    modal.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
      if (e.key === "Tab") {
        if (e.shiftKey) {
          if (document.activeElement === firstFocusable) {
            e.preventDefault();
            lastFocusable.focus();
          }
        } else {
         
          if (document.activeElement === lastFocusable) {
            e.preventDefault();
            firstFocusable.focus();
          }
        }
      }

      if (e.key === "Escape") {
        e.preventDefault();
        const bsModal = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(modal);
        bsModal.hide();
        if (lastFocusedElement) lastFocusedElement.focus();
      }
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
am5.ready(function() {

  // Create root
  var root = am5.Root.new("containerdata2");
  root.accessible = true;
  root._logo.dispose();

  root.setThemes([am5themes_Animated.new(root)]);

  // Create chart
  var chart = root.container.children.push(am5percent.SlicedChart.new(root, {
    layout: root.verticalLayout,
    role: "region",
    ariaLabel: "Funnel chart showing Aadhaar transaction breakdown"
  }));

  // Funnel series
  var series = chart.series.push(am5percent.FunnelSeries.new(root, {
    alignLabels: false,
    orientation: "vertical",
    valueField: "value",
    categoryField: "category",
    bottomRatio: 1
  }));

  // Replace with your actual values
  var total_transaction = 1.09535892E8;
  var aadhaar_txt = 1.07982743E8;
  var non_authenticated_txt = 1229496.0;
  var authenticated_txt = 323653.0;

  // Accessible slice labels
  series.slices.template.adapters.add("ariaLabel", function(text, target) {
    var dataItem = target.dataItem;
    if (dataItem) {
      var value = dataItem.get("value");
      var percent = ((value * 100) / total_transaction).toFixed(2);
      return dataItem.get("category") + " â " + percent + "%";
    }
    return text;
  });

//Each slice can be focused and spoken
  series.slices.template.setAll({
    focusable: true,
    role: "button",
    ariaHidden: false,
    cursorOverStyle: "pointer"
  });
  // Tooltip for visual users
  series.slices.template.adapters.add("title", function(text, target) {
    var dataItem = target.dataItem;
    if (dataItem) {
      var value = dataItem.get("value");
      var percent = ((value * 100) / total_transaction).toFixed(2);
      return dataItem.get("category") + ": " + percent + "%";
    }
    return text;
  });

  // Keyboard interaction for slices
  series.slices.template.events.on("keydown", function(ev) {
    if (ev.originalEvent.key === "Enter" || ev.originalEvent.key === " ") {
      var slice = ev.target;
      var dataItem = slice.dataItem;
      if (dataItem) {
        series.slices.each(function(s) { s.set("opacity", 1); });
        slice.set("opacity", 0.6);

        var value = dataItem.get("value");
        var percent = ((value * 100) / total_transaction).toFixed(2);
        document.getElementById("chartLiveRegion").textContent =
          dataItem.get("category") + ": " + percent + "%";
      }
      ev.originalEvent.preventDefault();
    }
  });

  // Set colors
  series.get("colors").set("colors", [
    am5.color("#507D32"), // Aadhaar Authenticated
    am5.color("#166988"), // Non-Authenticated
    am5.color("#CC6666")  // Other Mode Authenticated
  ]);

  // Set data
  series.data.setAll([
    { value: aadhaar_txt, category: "Aadhaar Authenticated" },
    { value: non_authenticated_txt, category: "Non-Authenticated" },
    { value: authenticated_txt, category: "Other Mode Authenticated" }
  ]);

  // Legend
  var legend = chart.children.push(am5.Legend.new(root, {
    centerX: am5.p50,
    x: am5.p50,
    marginTop: 15,
    marginBottom: 15,
    role: "list",
    ariaLabel: "Chart legend"
  }));

  legend.data.setAll(series.dataItems);

//Ensure legend item containers are positioned correctly
legend.itemContainers.template.setAll({
  focusable: true,
  tabindex: 0,
  role: "button", // change from listitem to button for better SR support
  ariaHidden: false,
  pointerEvents: "auto",
  visible: true
});

  // After chart and legend are ready, force recalculation
  chart.events.once("ready", function() {
      legend.itemContainers.each(function(container, index) {
          // set top positions manually based on index
          container.set("y", chart.height() + 5 + index * 30); 
          container.set("x", 100); // adjust left if needed
      });
  });


  // Legend ARIA label
legend.itemLabels.template.adapters.add("text", function(text, target) {
  var dataItem = target.dataItem;
  if (dataItem && dataItem.dataContext) {
    var category = dataItem.dataContext.category;
    var value = dataItem.dataContext.value;
    var percent = ((value * 100) / total_transaction).toFixed(2);
    return category + " (" + percent + "%)";
  }
  return text;
});
  // Update ARIA live region when legend items are clicked
legend.itemContainers.template.adapters.add("ariaLabel", function(label, target) {
  var dataItem = target.dataItem;
  if (dataItem && dataItem.dataContext) {
    var category = dataItem.dataContext.category;
    var value = dataItem.dataContext.value;
    var percent = ((value * 100) / total_transaction).toFixed(2);
    return category + ": " + percent + "%; Press Enter to toggle visibility";
  }
  return label;
});

//Fix: make Enter key work (not Alt+Enter)
legend.itemContainers.template.events.on("keydown", function(ev) {
  if (ev.originalEvent.key === "Enter" || ev.originalEvent.key === " ") {
    ev.originalEvent.preventDefault();
    ev.target.events.dispatch("click", { type: "click", target: ev.target });
    var dataItem = ev.target.dataItem;
    if (dataItem && dataItem.dataContext) {
      var category = dataItem.dataContext.category;
      var value = dataItem.dataContext.value;
      var percent = ((value * 100) / total_transaction).toFixed(2);
      document.getElementById("chartLiveRegion").textContent =
        category + " toggled. Current share " + percent + "%.";
    }
  }
});
  
  // Initial ARIA live region update
  setTimeout(function() {
    var messages = series.dataItems.map(function(di) {
      var category = di.get("category");
      var value = di.get("value");
      var percent = ((value * 100) / total_transaction).toFixed(2);
      return category + ": " + percent + "%";
    });
    document.getElementById("chartLiveRegion").textContent = messages.join(". ");
  }, 500);

//After chart render, adjust accessibility overlay positions if needed
  setTimeout(function() {
    document.querySelectorAll('div[role="listitem"][aria-label*="Aadhaar Authenticated"]').forEach(function(el) {
      el.style.top= "190px";
      el.style.left= "60px";
      el.style.width= "250px";

    });
    document.querySelectorAll('div[role="listitem"][aria-label*="Non-Authenticated"]').forEach(function(el) {
        el.style.top= "215px";
        el.style.left= "60px";
     });
  }, 1000); 
  // Animate chart
  series.appear(1000);
  chart.appear(1000, 100);

});
</script>

	     "Inter-StateTransaction": 0.0,
	     "Intra-stateTransaction": 0.10169400808199748,
	     "AadhaarTransaction": 11.680538086190133
	   },
	   
	    ];

	chart.data.sort((a, b) => a.StateName.localeCompare(b.StateName));
	chart.numberFormatter.numberFormat = "##.##";
	var categoryAxis = chart.xAxes.push(new am4charts.CategoryAxis());
	categoryAxis.renderer.labels.template.rotation = 290;
    categoryAxis.renderer.labels.template.wrap = true;
    categoryAxis.renderer.labels.template.truncate = false;
    categoryAxis.renderer.labels.template.maxWidth = 200;
	categoryAxis.dataFields.category = "StateName";
	categoryAxis.renderer.minGridDistance = 0;
	categoryAxis.renderer.labels.template.verticalCenter = "middle";
	categoryAxis.renderer.labels.template.horizontalCenter = "right";
	
	// Enable wrapping
	categoryAxis.renderer.labels.template.wrap = true;
	categoryAxis.renderer.labels.template.truncate = false;
	categoryAxis.renderer.labels.template.isMeasured = true;
	  
	var valueAxis = chart.yAxes.push(new am4charts.ValueAxis());
	valueAxis.title.text = "Monthly Transaction Performance Data";
	valueAxis.min = 0;
	valueAxis.max = 100;
	valueAxis.renderer.minGridDistance = 50;
	valueAxis.renderer.labels.template.adapter.add("text", function(text) {
	return text + "%";
	});
	var series = chart.series.push(new am4charts.ColumnSeries3D());
	series.dataFields.valueY = "Inter-StateTransaction";
	series.dataFields.categoryX = "StateName";
	series.name = "Inter-State Transaction";
	series.clustered = false;
	series.columns.template.tooltipText = "Inter-State Transaction: [bold]{valueY}[/]% {categoryX}";
	series.columns.template.fillOpacity = .8;
	series.configField = "config";
	var series2 = chart.series.push(new am4charts.ColumnSeries3D());
	series2.dataFields.valueY = "Intra-stateTransaction";
	series2.dataFields.categoryX = "StateName";
	series2.name = "Intra-state Transaction";
	series2.clustered = false;
	series2.columns.template.tooltipText = "Intra-state Transaction: [bold]{valueY}[/]% {categoryX}";
	var series3 = chart.series.push(new am4charts.ColumnSeries3D());
	series3.dataFields.valueY = "AadhaarTransaction";
	series3.dataFields.categoryX = "StateName";
	series3.name = "Aadhaar Transaction";
	series3.clustered = false;
	series3.columns.template.tooltipText = "Aadhaar Transaction:  [bold]{valueY}[/]% {categoryX}";
	series3.columns.template.fillOpacity = 0.9;
	chart.legend = new am4charts.Legend();

	  //  Accessibility: enable focus for 20+ items
	  chart.events.on("ready", function() {
	    if (chart.data.length >= 20) {
	      chart.series.each(function(series) {
	        series.columns.each(function(column) {
	          column.interactionsEnabled = true;
	          column.focusable = true;
	          column.role = "img";
	          column.readerTitle = series.name + ": " + column.dataItem.categoryX + " " +  parseFloat(column.dataItem.valueY).toFixed(2) + "%";
	        });
	      });
	    }
	  });

});
</script>
<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
  // Select all tables with class 'focus-table'
  const tables = document.querySelectorAll('.focus-table');

  tables.forEach(table => {
    table.querySelectorAll('th, td').forEach(cell => {
      cell.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');

      cell.addEventListener('focus', () => {
        cell.scrollIntoView({
          block: 'nearest',
          inline: 'nearest',
          behavior: 'smooth'
        });
      });
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
const trigger = document.querySelector('.tooltip-trigger');
const tooltip = document.querySelector('.tooltip-content');

// --- Show tooltip on hover/focus ---
trigger.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
  tooltip.classList.add('show');
});
trigger.addEventListener('focus', () => {
  tooltip.classList.add('show');
});

// --- Hide tooltip when mouse leaves or focus is lost ---
trigger.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
  tooltip.classList.remove('show');
});
trigger.addEventListener('blur', () => {
  tooltip.classList.remove('show');
});

// --- Make tooltip hoverable without disappearing ---
tooltip.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
  tooltip.classList.add('show');
});
tooltip.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
  tooltip.classList.remove('show');
});

// --- Allow ESC key to dismiss tooltip anytime ---
document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  if (e.key === 'Escape') {
    tooltip.classList.remove('show');
  }
});
</script>
